# OpenBiota project research catalogue — build specification v0.8.3

This single-file catalogue records the research publications, datasets, methods, software and reference resources cited in **BUILD_SPEC_v0.8.3.md**, including its targeted probiotic, synbiotic, substrate and compound evidence additions. Each entry identifies what the source contributes to OpenBiota and its principal institutions and geographic attribution. It does not certify that every proposed feature is already implemented.

**Coverage:** 328 consolidated publication records; 62 grouped data, software and reference-resource records; 2 separately identified preprint/thesis records; 7 product-label or formulation-identity records. A separate audit also preserves **82 explicit archive, sequence, protein and trial identifiers or grouped ranges**. The source audit accounts for **all 525 distinct literal URLs** in the specification plus **43 canonical links recovered from explicit publication identifiers**. Related paper, repository and dataset records remain separate where they describe different research objects. Repeated DOI, PubMed, PMC and publisher aliases are consolidated.

**Scope:** v0.8.3 only, as present on **22 September 2026**. Earlier build specifications are not imported wholesale. A source appearing in an earlier version remains here if v0.8.3 actually cites it. Exhaustiveness refers to the specified source document and its identifiable citations, not every publication on the topics.

## Reading and reusing the catalogue

- **Main institutions** are principal author affiliations (usually first and last authors), named developers or issuing organizations supported by primary publication, archive or maintainer metadata. They are not an exhaustive coauthor or funder list. Companies are included where appropriate. An archive or publisher is not automatically a study institution.
- **Geography basis:** `STUDY` = explicitly documented study setting; `AFFILIATION` = location of principal author institutions, without asserting that experiments occurred there; `MAINTAINER` = resource developer or issuing-organization location; `UNVERIFIED` = not established. Multiple countries may be listed.
- **How it informed OpenBiota** describes the proposed measurement, interpretation, intervention evidence, identity matching, validation or data acquisition. Human, animal, ex-vivo, in-vitro and computational evidence remain distinguishable. Positive, null and contradictory findings are retained.
- **Resource types** distinguish publications from software, raw sequences, models, reference databases, guidelines, trial registrations and commercial identity pages. A trial registration or product label is not an efficacy study. A correction, review, preprint and its eventual published version are not independent replications.
- **Institution names** are normalized throughout. Independent hospitals and companies remain distinct from affiliated universities. Historical legal entities are retained when a merger or renaming would obscure publication-time provenance. Unresolved information is explicitly marked rather than guessed.
- **Web conversion:** use the ten table headers as fields, preserve record IDs, and split semicolon-delimited institution/country fields into arrays for search and sorting. Keep provenance notes associated with the same ID. Open external links with `target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"`.

## Preprints and theses

These sources are retained because they are cited by v0.8.3. Their publication status is explicit; a linked later journal version is a version relationship, not an additional independent experiment.

| V0830001 | Effect of a Multi-Strain Probiotic on Growth Performance, Lipid Profile, Antioxidant Status and Immune Response in Piglets at Weaning | 2023 unreviewed preprint of a piglet probiotic study, with a subsequently published peer-reviewed version. | Used in the specification to classify AB687550–AB687552 as 16S records; preserve sequence-level provenance and replace preprint reliance with the published version where verified. | ICAR-Central Island Agricultural Research Institute | India | STUDY | Preprint — published version available | S083: §23.2 | [Resource](<https://www.preprints.org/manuscript/202309.0766>); [Published version](<https://doi.org/10.3390/fermentation9110970>); [Metadata / verification](<https://www.mdpi.com/2311-5637/9/11/970>) |
| V0830002 | Efficacy of Galactooliosaccharide (GOS) and/or Rhamnose-based Synbiotics in Enhancing Ecological Performance of Lactobacillus reuteri in the Human Gut and Characterization of Its GOS Metabolic System | 2015 doctoral dissertation on L. reuteri synbiotic persistence, metabolic activity and GOS utilization. | Provides author-level methodological detail for SYN005 and separates enhanced metabolic activity from the absence of improved post-dosing persistence. | University of Nebraska–Lincoln | United States | AFFILIATION | Doctoral dissertation | S083: §18 | [Resource](<https://digitalcommons.unl.edu/foodscidiss/62/>) |

## Corrections, identity limits and provenance notes

These notes are part of the catalogue record and should remain accessible from the corresponding row when converted into a webpage.

| Catalog record | Source-specific note |
| --- | --- |
| I0830001 | Audit-only developer identity. Official footer gives Delft, Netherlands. Website claims do not replace the distinct paper, genome deposit or verified finished-product identity. |
| I0830002 | Published reference database rather than efficacy research. API v9 documentation supplies contact and CC0 statement; label assertions remain manufacturer-supplied. |
| I0830003 | Audit-only identity bridge, not primary research. Page explicitly gives San Francisco headquarters and component strain identifiers; efficacy must be attributed to the separate publication. |
| I0830004 | Audit-only supporting identity, not research evidence. Manufacturer reports US production and University of Chicago links; these are not independent finished-product efficacy verification. Manufacturer domicile not inferred from production or collaborator locations. |
| I0830005 | Audit-only product identity. Official website gives Pontenure address; portfolio retrieval failed in this pass, so identity extraction relies on the specification’s prior audit, not a fresh PDF inspection. |
| I0830006 | Audit-only label provenance. Official ingredient page identifies the Canadian vendor and Japanese ingredient developer; manufacturer genome wording must not be applied to the linked partial 16S records. |
| I0830007 | Audit-only identity. Official contact gives Carlsbad, California. Manufacturer postbiotic terminology is not adopted as a scientific classification requiring inactivated microbes or components. |
| V0830001 | Original linked object is a preprint, explicitly not peer reviewed. Its page identifies a peer-reviewed version in Fermentation 2023, 9(11), 970, DOI 10.3390/fermentation9110970; published title differs: Effect of a Multi-Strain Probiotic on Growth Performance, Lipid Panel, Antioxidant Profile, and Immune Response in Andaman Local Piglets at Weaning. Published-version DOI is a related version, not an identical bibliographic alias. Study site and ethics body ICAR-CIARI, Port Blair, India verified; no full author-affiliation list inferred. |
| V0830002 | Repository identifies this as Rattanaprasert’s 2015 Ph.D. dissertation, not the 2014 journal article. Related journal DOI 10.1016/j.jff.2014.05.017; retain dissertation identity and do not count as an independent clinical study. |
| P0830001 | Computational case studies do not establish clinical efficacy. |
| P0830002 | Demonstrated in a cyanobacterial model; genomic distribution does not establish gut flux. |
| P0830003 | Female-rat experiment; no human efficacy evidence. |
| P0830004 | Human findings describe a subset, not all IBS-D. |
| P0830005 | Gnotobiotic model; individual fibers cannot be assumed to repair human mucus. |
| P0830006 | Published Cell Systems version, 19 February 2025. Distinguish from earlier APOLLO preprint PMID 37873072. Affiliations verified against official Galway publication record; catalogue institutions are not the countries sampled by the models. |
| P0830007 | Bilophila is sulfite-reducing; genus identity alone does not establish sulfate reduction. |
| P0830008 | Shared pathway acts on multiple aromatic substrates. |
| P0830009 | 89 participants; responder analyses and secondary endpoints require separate interpretation. |
| P0830010 | Malformed first-author metadata resolved using the primary article and USDA author record; no ordinary oral-product efficacy established. |
| P0830011 | Pathway capacity does not establish benefit from a particular food or supplement. |
| P0830012 | Catalytic reconstruction is distinct from host-level bile-acid effects. |
| P0830013 | Structural dispersal, prevention, and viability endpoints must remain separate. |
| P0830014 | Evidence from Listeria should not be assigned directly to another species. |
| P0830015 | 1,223 critically ill adults; early combined enteral and intravenous exposure. |
| P0830016 | 350 baseline participants, 219 evaluable at week six; retain attrition, funding and request-only participant-data availability. |
| P0830016 | Academic, clinical and commercial author affiliations verified in PubMed metadata. |
| P0830017 | Engineered-strain culture results do not establish retail-product effects. |
| P0830018 | Diagnostic association does not validate an interchangeable assay or treatment target. |
| P0830019 | Preclinical candidate; LAHUC is distinct from DSM14662/HM220. |
| P0830020 | Assay context matters; results do not quantify human cancer risk. |
| P0830021 | Human antibiotic association and experimental pathway evidence have different causal strength. |
| P0830022 | Potential and transcription do not directly quantify individual intestinal gas exposure. |
| P0830023 | Publication accessibility does not establish unrestricted dataset reuse. |
| P0830024 | Specific concentrate/extract; no human efficacy demonstrated. |
| P0830025 | Purified P9 effects are not interchangeable with whole-organism or retail-product effects. |
| P0830026 | Breath allyl methyl sulfide is a bioequivalence marker, not a gut-biofilm exposure measurement. |
| P0830027 | Hospital aliases consolidated; preparation, strains, and in vivo route need full-text extraction. |
| P0830028 | Static and dynamic device-surface models do not establish gut effects. |
| P0830029 | Rat findings require human validation; mixture prevents single-strain attribution. |
| P0830030 | Genotype detection does not by itself establish phenotypic resistance. |
| P0830031 | Structural classes and tested substrates must accompany functional claims. |
| P0830032 | 98 participants; strain identity and regional subgroup results require separate interpretation. |
| P0830033 | Gastrointestinal delivery and exposure remain unestablished. |
| P0830034 | Biofilm prevention does not establish eradication of mature biofilms. |
| P0830035 | Urinary isolates; viability and detection-limit evidence are needed for eradication claims. |
| P0830036 | Small antibiotic-depleted mouse groups; prophylactic gavage before challenge. |
| P0830037 | Eugenol is not interchangeable with clove oil; no gut model. |
| P0830038 | Vegetative-cell growth assay; no human treatment or biofilm evidence. |
| P0830039 | Negative organism results and extract concentration remain integral to interpretation. |
| P0830040 | Correction notice, not independent research; no abstract supplied. |
| P0830041 | Distinct correction identifier; no abstract supplied. |
| P0830042 | 247 participants; use the publisher's corrected version. |
| P0830044 | Oral delivery and human efficacy of the active extract were not tested. |
| P0830045 | Immortalized and primary mouse cells; duration determines response direction. |
| P0830046 | Trait coverage varies among strains; inferred traits are not direct activity measurements. |
| P0830047 | 48 participants; PICRUSt functional predictions are not directly measured gene activity. |
| P0830048 | Community abundance does not establish direct antibacterial activity. |
| P0830049 | Affiliation geography differs from the Bangladeshi trial population; 62 infants in the pilot. |
| P0830050 | Bile-acid product profiles depend on enzyme and substrate context. |
| P0830051 | Cohort correlations and cell-receptor activity do not establish clinical benefit. |
| P0830052 | Primary publisher and Europe PMC affiliations agree. Lower-evidence families use limited-reference homolog detection; family presence and operational guilds are not direct enzyme activity or human clinical validation. U0055 is the associated repository, not the paper. |
| P0830053 | 40 participants; 13/20 versus 4/20 eradications after at least four prior failures. |
| P0830054 | Historical platform paper does not establish current service availability. |
| P0830055 | Six-week rat feeding experiment; fractions are not interchangeable. |
| P0830056 | 10% dietary powder in male rats; no human-dose inference. |
| P0830057 | Publisher abstract explicitly identifies a review. |
| P0830058 | Simulated mild-constipation context; no clinical efficacy demonstrated. |
| P0830060 | Short consecutive periods; diet and order effects limit generalization. |
| P0830061 | Enzyme capacity does not itself establish harmful mucus erosion in a host. |
| P0830062 | Prevention assays do not establish mature-biofilm eradication. |
| P0830063 | Ten randomized hypercholesterolemic adults plus four additional normocholesterolemic participants; surrogate endpoints. |
| P0830064 | 263 enrolled, 163 paired samples; diet and supplement effects are not cleanly separable. |
| P0830065 | 20 men per group; habitual diet comparison, not randomized feeding. |
| P0830066 | Pectin-rich forage differs from isolated inulin. |
| P0830067 | 127 completers; lipid biomarkers do not establish fewer cardiovascular events. |
| P0830068 | 114 completers; six-week treatment; biomarkers rather than cardiovascular events. |
| P0830069 | One positive trial is not a comprehensive assessment of the strain. |
| P0830070 | Exogenous para-hydroxyphenylacetate and expression context determine pathway induction. |
| P0830071 | Primary article reports Osaka University affiliations and Japan study setting. CDC is the journal host, not an author institution. Published 2024; five identified bacteremia cases genetically linked to probiotic derivatives. |
| P0830072 | Detection in healthy human plasma does not establish causal human effects. |
| P0830073 | Six donors sampled at two ages; laboratory synergy is not clinical efficacy. |
| P0830074 | Production in culture does not quantify host neurotransmitter exposure or clinical effects. |

Primary PDF page 2 explicitly identifies rapporteur laboratory JRC Geel, Belgium. |
| R0830024 | Document dated 12 May 2026 for July 2026 Pharmacy Compounding Advisory Committee. FDA author offices and Silver Spring address appear on page 4. Briefing explicitly is not a final legal determination. |
| R0830025 | Creator Francesco Asnicar identified by archive; affiliation corroborated by associated study. |
| R0830026 | Creator affiliations verified in DataCite; detailed eligibility and availability remain dataset-specific. |
| R0830027 | Institutional attribution is to the maintaining Raes laboratory, not a publisher or data host. |
| R0830029 | Maintainer attribution follows first and corresponding authors’ IIIT-Delhi affiliation; collaborating paper affiliations are not conflated with code maintenance. |
| R0830030 | Maintainer attribution reuses the supported earlier catalogue. Metabolic potential is distinct from direct metabolite measurement. |
| R0830031 | Current repository supersedes the unmaintained Aarhus-hosted site. Current maintenance geography is Australia; original method-paper affiliations are separate. Repository redistribution license remains to be verified. |
| R0830032 | Official tools page and primary publication verified; publication explicitly links the ZIP. Mutable archive requires a recorded content hash and extracted inventory. Exact archive redistribution terms and complete download were not verified here. |
| R0830032 | Primary paper identifies University of Parma developers and downloadable ZIP. Current binary retrieval and dataset-specific redistribution license were not verified; article license alone does not establish genome reuse rights. |
| R0830033 | Maintainer attribution is the Segata laboratory. Published species association ranks are contextual associations. |
| R0830034 | CC BY 4.0 archive; not a validated individual human response predictor. |
| R0830035 | Repository provides Apache-2.0 workflows; built database access is separately described by maintainers. Country attribution follows principal software authors in the primary paper. |
| R0830036 | Current resource is administered by EMBL-EBI; historical Sanger hosting is not current maintenance. |
| R0830037 | Replaying released outputs is distinct from solving the model or measuring a user outcome. |
| R0830038 | Original forgemia URL redirects to the INRAE forge. Benchmark source is Meslier et al. 2022, also reused by the Sylph paper; its provenance is not the Sylph authors’ affiliation. |
| R0830039 | Maintainer attribution is the EMBL Heidelberg Bork group; partner paper affiliations are recorded in the publication entry. |
| R0830040 | Zenodo names Christian Diener and Institute for Systems Biology. Listed species artifact is 264.4 MB, MD5 6a13da2a3fd9b1bc059987d6344f32b6. Country describes archive maintainer; AGORA2 source-paper affiliations span additional countries. Model archive not downloaded. |
| R0830041 | All three official documentation pages verified. Country denotes the maintaining institution, not the geographic origin of deposited samples. |
| R0830042 | API verified 22 September 2026: sponsor Helaina; collaborator Biofortis; site Addison, Illinois; hasResults=false. Last posted update 25 June 2025; still lists RECRUITING, so current actual recruitment/completion cannot be inferred from the stale status. |
| R0830043 | Reference synthesis rather than a structured strain–target research database. |
| R0830044 | The specification uses this source for dose/exposure context, not proof of zinc-carnosine efficacy. |
| R0830045 | Current 2026 paper affiliations establish principal developers in China; the historical Singapore attribution was not carried forward. Website returned 403 during this pass; binary downloads and specific reuse terms remain unverified. |
| R0830046 | Spec pins commit 184f1cc99aefed722e20eb00eda082348a064c4e. Wrapper GPL-3.0 and bundled Java academic noncommercial license must remain distinct. |
| R0830047 | Sequence support does not quantify host GLP-1. |
| R0830048 | Author affiliations include academic, hospital and commercial contributors. The specification identifies 44 commensals plus 29 potential probiotics and 28 edible substrates, including minerals. Archive not independently re-downloaded; not all tested substrates are established prebiotics. |
| R0830049 | BioProject directly names Hubei Normal University and raw sequence reads, multispecies scope, 20 SRA experiments, V3–V4 16S and July 2026 registration. Geography is submitting institution affiliation, not NCBI location. |
| R0830050 | Repository verified. Preserve snapshot-specific counts and hash; publication and repository counts differ. Laboratory trait fields do not establish human efficacy, and exact assay details require the cited papers. JSON binary content was not independently rehashed here. |
| R0830050 | GitHub README and MIT license verified. Article and README counts differ, so retain snapshot-specific counts. Principal developer institution is from the database paper. |
| R0830051 | Principal developer affiliations are in Hong Kong. Live service operation and a public API/full dump were not verified; paper documents CSV results export. Free access is not a commercial redistribution license. |
| R0830052 | Pinned repository revision retained from the specification. Primary paper and archive provenance verified; pinned source URL could not be independently fetched during catalogue verification. Some source inputs may require requests. |
| R0830052 | Raw URL is a commit directory prefix, not a standalone downloadable file. Repository README identifies Quinn-Bohmann and Gibbons analysis; exact relative files and SHA256 pins are specified in BUILD_SPEC section 15.6. |
| R0830053 | Source-specific reuse metadata must be retained; no blanket public-domain assumption. Geography describes the database maintainer, not the originating assays. |
| R0830054 | Database maintainer attribution only; individual publication affiliations must be recorded independently. |
| R0830055 | NCBI is the reference-record maintainer, not the study institution. Spec identifies 624 amino acids, a reference MAG rather than WL68 isolate, and an unresolved exact study construct. |
| R0830056 | Current managers are Xinpeng Zhang, Haidong Yi and Yanbin Yin. GPL-3.0 code and data-distribution terms are distinct; optional SignalP6/DeepTMHMM binaries have separate terms. |
| R0830057 | Predicted production is distinct from measured stool or host SCFA concentrations. |
| R0830058 | Institutions and countries describe UniProt consortium maintainers, not the P9 experimental study. Spec specifies 748 amino acids and an exact sequence hash. |
| R0830059 | Official page names Reading and Leeds collaborations, launch on 1 March 2025 and loss of ongoing update funding. UK attribution concerns the named academic collaborators; AEProbio domicile not established. |
| R0830060 | Academic affiliations shown; primary paper also lists industry collaborators in Denmark and Finland. Figure S1 is experimental screening evidence, not a human treatment trial; binary supplement not re-downloaded. |
| R0830061 | Institutional attribution follows the linked primary publication; archive creator uses a personal GitHub handle. |
| R0830062 | Country is the issuing organization secretariat location in Milwaukee; the guideline has international authors and global scope. No open-data redistribution license or public API established. |

A project accession identifies the deposited experiment; it is not automatically an isolate genome or a whole-genome assay. Accession ranges are labeled explicitly.
